# Reconciled Investigative Public-Record API Catalogue — Volume 2 of 5

Systems SYS-0268 through SYS-0565. This is a companion source to `investigative_api_catalogue_00_guide.txt`. Each system record remains intact with its access points, coverage mappings, ranking, and verification material. Stable identifiers should be used when joining records across volumes.

## SYS-0268 — Office of the Superintendent of Financial Institutions open financial data

- **Publisher:** [not recorded]
- **Official domain:** osfi-bsif.gc.ca
- **Normalized jurisdiction:** Canada
- **Coarse record family:** Sanctions, regulation & enforcement
- **Official or non-official status:** Official / public authority
- **Geographic coverage:** Canada; federally regulated banks, insurers, pension plans and trust/loan companies
- **Access modes:** Bulk / download
- **Representative URL:** https://www.osfi-bsif.gc.ca/en/data-forms/open-government-osfi-financial-data
- **Source-system confidence:** Medium

### Editorial ranking
- **Rank:** 466
- **Final active:** YES
- **Verification status:** BASELINE_CARRIED_FORWARD
- **Verification date:** 2026-08-11
- **Total score:** 77
- **Component scores:** HNWI relevance: 5; Source authority: 5; Person/entity linkage: 5; Legal reuse confidence: 1

### Access points

#### AP-0269 — Office of the Superintendent of Financial Institutions open financial data
- **Layer:** Established
- **Original ID:** SRC-269
- **Normalized record family:** Sanctions, regulation & enforcement
- **Normalized jurisdiction:** Canada
- **Access-point type:** Bulk / download
- **Original access method:** Machine-readable bulk downloads from the official open-data page
- **Representative access URL:** https://www.osfi-bsif.gc.ca/en/data-forms/open-government-osfi-financial-data
- **Documentation URL:** https://www.osfi-bsif.gc.ca/en/data-forms/open-government-osfi-financial-data
- **Investigative relevance or methodology:** Institution-level regulatory financial data for capital, assets, mortgages, deposits and insurance-market analysis.
- **Cost or eligibility:** No fee
- **Refresh cadence:** Monthly and quarterly, table-dependent
- **Useful join fields:** Institution name/code; reporting period; financial measure
- **Main weakness:** Confidentiality and consolidation rules limit detail; the legacy Financial Data system is scheduled to close on 4 September 2026.
- **Legal tier:** A
- **Overall score out of 5:** 4.7

#### AP-0270 — CanadaBuys procurement and contracting datasets
- **Layer:** Established
- **Original ID:** SRC-270
- **Normalized record family:** Procurement, spending & aid
- **Normalized jurisdiction:** Canada
- **Access-point type:** API / web service; Bulk / download
- **Original access method:** Bulk downloads; pilot Open Contracting Data Standard JSON
- **Representative access URL:** https://canadabuys.canada.ca/en/procurement-and-contracting-data
- **Documentation URL:** https://canadabuys.canada.ca/en/procurement-and-contracting-data
- **Investigative relevance or methodology:** Tender notices, award notices, contract history, standing offers and supply arrangements for supplier, commodity and agency investigations.
- **Cost or eligibility:** No fee
- **Refresh cadence:** Ongoing or periodic by dataset
- **Useful join fields:** Notice ID; solicitation number; contract ID; supplier; buyer; commodity code
- **Main weakness:** Fields and historical coverage differ among procurement systems; amendments and duplicate notices need reconciliation.
- **Legal tier:** A
- **Overall score out of 5:** 4.8

#### AP-0271 — Federal proactive publication — contracts
- **Layer:** Established
- **Original ID:** SRC-271
- **Normalized record family:** Procurement, spending & aid
- **Normalized jurisdiction:** Canada
- **Access-point type:** API / web service; Bulk / download
- **Original access method:** CSV bulk file; JSON schema; XLSX dictionary
- **Representative access URL:** https://open.canada.ca/data/en/dataset/d8f85d91-7dec-4fd1-8055-483b77225d8b
- **Documentation URL:** https://open.canada.ca/data/en/dataset/d8f85d91-7dec-4fd1-8055-483b77225d8b
- **Investigative relevance or methodology:** Consolidated contracts over $10,000 plus aggregates for smaller contracts, with reporting entities, vendors, values, dates and descriptions.
- **Cost or eligibility:** No fee
- **Refresh cadence:** Quarterly/ongoing submissions
- **Useful join fields:** Vendor name; reference number; organization; date; commodity; value
- **Main weakness:** Entity names are not normalised and reports derive from institution submissions; amendments require longitudinal handling.
- **Legal tier:** A
- **Overall score out of 5:** 4.9

#### AP-0274 — House of Commons of Canada open data
- **Layer:** Established
- **Original ID:** SRC-274
- **Normalized record family:** Politics, law & archives
- **Normalized jurisdiction:** Canada
- **Access-point type:** Unstructured / unresolved
- **Original access method:** Official machine-readable datasets and query services
- **Representative access URL:** https://www.ourcommons.ca/en/open-data
- **Documentation URL:** https://www.ourcommons.ca/en/open-data
- **Investigative relevance or methodology:** Members, roles, expenditures, party standings, constituencies, bills, votes, debates, committees, evidence, publications and petitions.
- **Cost or eligibility:** No fee
- **Refresh cadence:** Sitting-day or dataset-dependent
- **Useful join fields:** Member ID; constituency; Parliament/session; bill; vote; committee; document
- **Main weakness:** Parliamentary language and identifiers require careful session-aware joins; Senate coverage differs.
- **Legal tier:** A

#### AP-0275 — LEGISinfo bills open data
- **Layer:** Established
- **Original ID:** SRC-275
- **Normalized record family:** Politics, law & archives
- **Normalized jurisdiction:** Canada
- **Access-point type:** API / web service; Search / web portal
- **Original access method:** JSON and XML exports from overview, search and bill pages
- **Representative access URL:** https://www.parl.ca/legisinfo/en/help
- **Documentation URL:** https://www.parl.ca/legisinfo/en/help
- **Investigative relevance or methodology:** Machine-readable bill metadata, stage history, sponsors, votes and publications for tracking policy change and legislative timing.
- **Cost or eligibility:** No fee
- **Refresh cadence:** As parliamentary business changes
- **Useful join fields:** Bill number; Parliament/session; sponsor; stage; date
- **Main weakness:** It is bill tracking, not a consolidated laws database; session boundaries and reintroduced bills need care.
- **Legal tier:** A

#### AP-0277 — Supreme Court of Canada judgments feeds
- **Layer:** Established
- **Original ID:** SRC-277
- **Normalized record family:** Politics, law & archives
- **Normalized jurisdiction:** Canada
- **Access-point type:** API / web service; Feed / stream
- **Original access method:** RSS and JSON feeds
- **Representative access URL:** https://decisions.scc-csc.ca/scc-csc/en/rss/index.do
- **Documentation URL:** https://decisions.scc-csc.ca/scc-csc/en/rss/index.do
- **Investigative relevance or methodology:** Official RSS and JSON feeds provide low-friction monitoring of judgments and case-law developments.
- **Cost or eligibility:** No fee
- **Refresh cadence:** As judgments are released
- **Useful join fields:** Neutral citation; docket; parties; decision date
- **Main weakness:** Feeds may expose only recent items; full reasoning and subsequent corrections must be read at source.
